# P9.3 red and green records

Plan: the approved P9.3 design (v6) and test plan (v6), baseline lane,
steps B1 to B3. Each new test in this file ran twice: once with its named
break applied (red) and once on the committed code (green). The break is
one exact text replacement, shown as a diff, applied to a copy of the
tree and reverted. The red lines are the failing assertion and the
nextest summary. The green line is the nextest summary after the revert.

Reproduce one record:

```sh
mise exec -- cargo nextest run --locked --no-fail-fast \
  -p jackin-console --test <file> -E 'test(=<name>)'
```

Apply the break first, run, revert it, run again. Breaks touch library
source only, never an approved baseline. After the revert, touch the file
so the build sees it as changed.

Gate after each step: `cargo nextest run --locked --all-targets`,
`cargo clippy --locked --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `cargo fmt --check`,
and an empty `git diff --stat origin/feature/openshell-native --
tests/functional/baselines apps/jackin/tests/functional/baselines`.
## B1: fixtures in the library, World::new (BX1, BX12)

Tests: `apps/jackin/tests/world.rs`.

#### `every_scenario_builds_a_world_that_names_it`: world_for FirstUse returns the populated world

```diff

-            let mut w = base_world(scenario);
-            w.discovery_pending = true;
```

```text
assertion failed: world_for(Scenario::FirstUse).workspaces.is_empty()
Summary 1 test run: 0 passed, 1 failed, 4 skipped
```

Green (exit 0):

```text
Summary 1 test run: 1 passed, 4 skipped
```

## B4-0: the fixed state, the refusal slot, reason widths, the fixed-controls page (BX10 library part, BX17)

Tests: `apps/jackin/tests/fixed.rs`, `reason.rs`, `account_data.rs` and, for the page, `tests/functional/showcase.rs` (`showcase_fixed_controls_*`, `showcase_page_fixedcontrols`). The in-process tests render library widgets into a test buffer and drive them with keys and clicks. The page tests run the `showcase` binary in a pseudo-terminal. Each break ran in a copy of the tree. The records of `a_blocked_edit_leaves_the_other_actions_open` add to its B3 records; the test now sets a `Reason` and checks the menu border.

Reproduce a page record with `cargo nextest run --locked --test functional --no-tests fail -E 'test(=showcase::<name>)'`. The page baseline `showcase_page_fixedcontrols.tui.json` is new and was accepted with the Tuiscotti CLI; the break that renames the checkbox goes red against it.

One break was dropped: making the refusal use the error tone does not turn the page test red, because the error status clears on the next key and `advance_clock` is a paste. The in-process test `a_refusal_posts_the_reason_alone_in_a_neutral_tone` owns that claim.

## B4-0 review round: the flaky leak test and the fixes behind it

`showcase_fixed_controls_refuse_with_a_reason_and_never_leak_it` failed on the
unmodified commit `a1435ba`: 3 failures in 21 isolated runs, and 7 of 12 when run
with the other two fixed-control tests. The failing step was always `key space`
on the `Typed key` radio option, `timed out: status reads Not available · managed
by policy`.

Cause: the page returned `Outcome::Consumed` after a refusal, the showcase host did
not turn a posted status into `Changed`, and the runtime redraws only on `Changed`.
The new status painted only if a pointer flash or a status expiry repainted first.
The jackin console was not affected (its `apply_requests` returns `Changed`).

Fix, in the shared layer so no page has to remember it: `shell::handle` compares the
status line (text and tone) before and after each input and ORs in `Changed`; the
showcase host returns `Changed` when a page posts any request. The slot is also
cleared at the start of `handle`, not only at the end.

The tests that pin this round (keyboard-only redraw, the ListItem paths, the exact
neutral tone, the sticky status, the Dialog fixed action) are recorded in the next
section.

## B4-0 fix round: the review fixes, the library tests and the page tests

Plan: the B4-0 fix test plan, rows T1 to T15 and the unpinned vocabulary strings, as amended by its review (A1 to A16). Each record below is one break and one test: the break is applied to the tree by a script, the named test runs red, the file is restored and the same test runs green. A break is one edit, or a named group of edits when the code has two guards for the same behaviour (the records say which). Every red line is the assertion that failed, with its expected and actual values. Breaks touch library or console source only, never an approved baseline.

The tests of the library's own types moved out of `apps/jackin/tests` into the library's root `tests/`: `tests/fixed.rs` and `tests/reason.rs` (the 15 tests that were `apps/jackin/tests/fixed.rs` and `reason.rs`, minus `the_shell_clears_an_untaken_refusal_at_the_end_of_each_input`, which is rewritten on a `shell::App` double in `tests/shell.rs`), with the helpers in `tests/common/mod.rs`. The records of the section "B4-0" above were taken when those files lived under `apps/jackin/tests/`; the test names are unchanged and the line numbers are those of that location. A move has no break; the 15 moved tests run in `tests/fixed.rs` and `tests/reason.rs` and pass.

Run a library record with `cargo nextest run --locked -p junie-tui --test <file> -E 'test(=<name>)'` and a console record with `-p jackin-console --test <file>`.

Rows written and kept: every test in these records turns red under at least one break.

Rows dropped, because no break turns them red or the premise is gone:

- T3, 70,000 reasons in a loop and a `Reason` sent to another thread (A3): after the slot became a plain `Option<Reason>` neither exercises code that can break. Kept: a later refusal replaces an earlier one, and the slot belongs to its thread. The old break (a `Cell<Option<u16>>` index table) does not compile and is not a red.
- T4, a reason raised and taken inside `on_input` is unaffected (A4): no edit to `shell::handle` changes it, because the host takes the slot before `handle` can clear it.
- T8, the key table over both arrow modes (A7): the existing select test holds the break, with `Left` added to the ignored keys and a `Step` select added.
- T9, `short` against `reason_room` (A8): it measures a string the test built; the break "render `text` in the border" is red in `a_blocked_edit_leaves_the_other_actions_open` (record in the section "B4-0").
- T13, the full reason at 80x24 (A10): the named break is already red in the existing test and no code caps the status.
- T14, the separate keyboard-only PTY test (A6): merged into the walk test. The plan premise that `advance_clock` expires a status in the showcase is wrong: the showcase has no `advance_clock`, so the clock step is only a paste. The walk runs with sticky reasons and clears the status with one key (`f9`); a 5 s expiry in real time is `showcase_fixed_controls_reason_fades_after_five_seconds`.
- `lifecycle.restored`, `status.purged`, the nine-status table and `destination_heading` (A14): shadowed, unreachable or already pinned. They are recorded in `docs/audits/2026-11-03-fixed-controls-showcase-page.md`.
- `unpushed` in the banned-word list (A14): subsumed by the existing `pushed`. `discard` and `{state}` were added.
- The launch picker for the current directory (section 5 of the plan): no key sequence in the `Configured` rule reaches a current-directory scope that shows `no default role`; the new 80-column assertion on the existing picker test carries the N1 text.

Survivors worth knowing: `p-select-press` and `p-select-click` (each alone) leave all page tests green, as the review predicted; `d-guard` alone leaves `a_dialog_refuses_a_fixed_action_by_every_route` green because the other routes refuse before `finish` is reached.


### Dialog: a fixed action refuses on every route

Tests: `tests/fixed.rs`. The finish guard and the per-route breaks together: a route that still goes through `Button::activate` is also caught by the guard in `Dialog::finish`, so a route break removes the guard as well and restores the route as it was before the fix.

### Showcase page: keyboard and pointer refusals in a real terminal

Tests: `tests/functional/showcase.rs`. Reproduce a record with `cargo nextest run --locked -p junie-tui --test functional --no-tests fail -E 'test(=showcase::<name>)'`. The walk test runs with `--motion paused` and `fixedcontrols.status=sticky`, so no timer can repaint for the shell and the status clears with one key. The two single-route Select breaks (`p-select-press`, `p-select-click`) leave every test green, because a pointer click delivers a press and a click and both refuse; the in-process assertions of `a_fixed_select_refuses_open_arrows_typing_and_clicks` own them.

## B4-0 fix round 2: the last red and green findings

Source: the independent red and green check of `7ed1666` (verdict CHANGES_REQUIRED, findings F1 to F5 and notes N1, N2). Each record below is one break and one test: the break is applied by a script, the named test runs red, the file is restored, and the same test runs green. Breaks touch library or console source only, never an approved baseline.

- F1: `tests/shell.rs` gains `the_same_tone_with_other_text_is_a_change`. Two neutral reasons in a row differ in their text only; the redraw rule must report `Changed`.
- F2: a code defect. The GitHub dialog posted "Copied the GitHub URL" when no repository matched and nothing was copied. `capsule.rs` now posts the new `Vocabulary::github_no_url` in the error tone when there is no URL, in the demo words and the openshell words alike. A demo flow with a matching repository is untouched, so no approved baseline moved. `the_github_dialog_copies_nothing_without_a_matching_repository` no longer pins the false status; it keeps one repository that does not match the instance, so a match-anything break turns it red. Its records, and the two Copy URL records it shares with the section "Console: GitHub dialog and mount rows", are rewritten for the new code in that section.
- F3: the capture count of `the_unexported_word_replaces_unpushed` is eight, not ten (corrected in its section). The 10-session assertion of `the_purge_dialog_follows_the_words` now has its record below (a session-count cap).
- F4: `a_prompt_refuses_a_fixed_primary_when_the_field_commits` and `a_typed_acknowledgement_refuses_a_fixed_gated_action` assert the outcome of the refused Enter: `Consumed` when the action is fixed and `Changed` when it is live. A guard that returns `Ignored` lets the key bubble to the host, and the outcome is observable through `Dialog::on_key`.
- F5: `an_unchecked_fixed_row_does_not_block_deselecting_all` presses `a` twice with an unchecked fixed row. The review's probe was green on `7ed1666`: `RowSelection::tri_state` already filters out the rows that cannot be selected (`src/core/selection.rs`), and `toggle_all` passes the same predicate. No code changed. The test only pins that filter, and the break removes it.
- N1: accepted as it is. `the_shell_clears_an_untaken_refusal_at_the_end_of_each_input` and `the_shell_clears_a_stale_refusal_before_each_input` in `tests/shell.rs` own the slot clear. The PTY test `showcase_fixed_controls_refuse_with_a_reason_and_never_leak_it` cannot produce a stale reason, because every raise in its sequence is taken in the same input, so a `handle` that never clears the slot leaves it green. This record does not claim PTY coverage of the slot clear.
- Line numbers: the records of the section "Console: lifecycle, purge, stop, project count, trust and env words" and the sections after it quote `apps/jackin/tests/vocabulary.rs` lines from before this round. Every line after `the_github_dialog_copies_nothing_without_a_matching_repository` moved down by 16.

Run a library record with `cargo nextest run --locked -p junie-tui --test <file> -E 'test(=<name>)'` and a console record with `cargo nextest run --locked -p jackin-console --features fixtures --test vocabulary -E 'test(=<name>)'`.

```diff
-            .map(|(text, tone)| (text.to_owned(), tone))
+            .map(|(_, tone)| (String::new(), tone))
```

## B4a: the Fixed data, the openshell-fixed scenario and the editor Fixed paths (BX10)

Tests: `apps/jackin/tests/functional/jackin.rs`, seven PTY key tests, one per
refusal, and four baselines. Run them with:

```sh
cargo nextest run --locked -p jackin-console --test functional -E 'test(jackin_fixed)'
```

| Test | Refusal | Baseline | Breaks |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `jackin_fixed_keep_awake_refuses_by_click_and_key` | `keep_awake` | `jackin_fixed_editor_general` | S1, S2, KA1, KA2, GP3, T1, T2 |
| `jackin_fixed_git_pull_refuses_by_key_and_click` | `git_pull` | `jackin_fixed_editor_general_refused` | S1, S2, GP1, GP2, GP3 |
| `jackin_fixed_dirty_exit_refuses_the_list_by_key_and_click` | dirty exit | none | S1, S2, DE1, DE2 |
| `jackin_fixed_working_directory_refuses_the_picker_by_key_and_click` | working directory | none | S1, S2, KA2, WD1, WD2, WD3 |
| `jackin_fixed_project_row_keeps_its_paint_and_refuses_every_edit` | Project row, Add row | `jackin_fixed_editor_project` | S1, PR1 to PR7 |
| `jackin_fixed_workspace_account_toggle_refuses_and_prefer_stays_live` | workspace account toggle | `jackin_fixed_editor_accounts` | S1, AC1 to AC4 |
| `jackin_fixed_last_allowed_role_cannot_be_unchecked` | last allowed role | none | LR1, LR2 |

Each record below is one break in production code, the failing assertion, and
the green run after the revert. The last-role refusal posts its text with
`cx.status` and not through the refusal slot, so S1 leaves that test green
(1 passed in the S1 summary). The data breaks T1 and T2 change a reason text
in `Fixed::openshell()`: the tests read the text from the same function, so
only the frozen baseline turns red. A refusal posted as a sticky status
instead of a 5 s status is not caught here: the harness clock request is a
paste, and a paste clears a sticky status, so `status_expired` cannot tell
the two apart. The status tests of the library (`tests/status.rs`) pin it.
Breaks touch library source and the fixture data only, never an approved
baseline.

Five assertion classes are pinned by one test each:

- the F10 gate: `the Project row stays legible` (contrast of the destination,
  mode and source cells against their background is at least 4.5:1, PR2) and
  `the Add row is drawn fixed` (PR6);
- the reason beside the control (KA1, GP1, DE2, WD2, PR4);
- the refusal by key and by click (KA1, GP1, DE1, WD1, PR1, PR5, AC1, LR1);
- the footer hints that omit a key that only refuses (KA2, WD3, PR3, AC2);
- the live neighbour: `p` still binds (AC3) and a role can be unchecked while
  another stays allowed (LR2).

## B4b: the Settings and env Fixed paths (BX10, BX13)

Tests: `apps/jackin/tests/functional/jackin.rs`, eight PTY key tests and six
baselines, and `apps/jackin/tests/fixed_settings.rs`, ten library data tests (eight at B4b, two more in the fix round).
Run them with:

```sh
cargo nextest run --locked -p jackin-console --test functional -E 'test(jackin_fixed_settings) | test(jackin_fixed_env) | test(jackin_fixed_workspace_env)'
cargo nextest run --locked -p jackin-console --test fixed_settings
```

| Test | Refusal or rule | Baseline | Breaks |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `jackin_fixed_settings_commits_refuse_by_key_and_click` | Co-author and DCO boxes | `jackin_fixed_settings_commits` | S1, S2, CO1 to CO5, TX1 |
| `jackin_fixed_settings_global_mounts_refuse_every_add` | global Mounts Add row | `jackin_fixed_settings_mounts` | S1, S2, MO1 to MO5, TX6 |
| `jackin_fixed_settings_trust_refuses_the_toggle_and_shows_the_source` | Trust toggle, `o` | `jackin_fixed_settings_trust` | S1, S2, TR1 to TR3, TR5 to TR7, TX2 |
| `jackin_fixed_settings_agents_refuse_every_row` | every Agents row | `jackin_fixed_settings_agents` | S1, S2, AG1 to AG7, TX3 |
| `jackin_fixed_settings_role_overrides_refuse_and_the_scope_lists_global` | global Role overrides, scope select | `jackin_fixed_settings_env` | S1, RO1 to RO7, ES5, TX4 |
| `jackin_fixed_env_sources_refuse_the_radio_options_and_the_p_key` | env sources 2 and 3, `p` | `jackin_fixed_env_form` | S1, L1, L2, ES1 to ES4, TX5 |
| `jackin_fixed_env_form_refuses_a_credential_reference_and_notes_a_secret_name` | `op://` and `openshell:resolve:` values, secret note | none | S2, ER1 to ER3, ER5, ER7 |
| `jackin_fixed_workspace_env_keeps_the_role_scope_live_and_refuses_the_sources` | workspace env: role scope stays live, `p` refuses | none | S1, ES3, ES5 |
| `every_fixed_text_fits_the_surface_that_shows_it` | reason and note widths | none | D1 |
| `a_credential_reference_is_refused_in_any_letter_case_after_leading_space` | the value rule | none | ER5 to ER7 |
| `only_a_name_with_a_secret_word_gets_the_note` | the name rule | none | ER4 |
| `a_trust_source_stays_until_the_next_key_and_a_refusal_expires` | sticky `o`, 5 s refusal | none | TR4 |
| `the_demo_settings_keep_every_control` | demo Settings unchanged | none | DM1 to DM3 |
| `the_demo_env_form_keeps_its_sources_and_saves_what_it_is_given` | demo env form unchanged | none | DM4 |
| `the_demo_env_form_requires_a_value` | the value input is required in the demo form | none | BF5 |
| `a_rejected_rename_leaves_the_original_variable_alone`, `the_demo_env_form_stays_open_on_a_rejected_rename_and_changes_nothing` | a rejected rename stages nothing; the form stays open on Save | none | BF6, BF8 |
| `the_demo_env_form_saves_a_plain_value_that_starts_with_op` | demo accepts `op://` as plain | none | ER8 |

What the records show and what they cannot:

- A sticky status cannot be told from a 5 s status in a PTY test: the harness
  steps the clock with a paste, and a paste clears a sticky status. The library
  data test `a_trust_source_stays_until_the_next_key_and_a_refusal_expires`
  drives the real `App` with ticks instead (TR4).
- The reason texts are read from `Fixed::openshell()` by the tests, so a change
  of text only turns the frozen baselines red (TX1 to TX6). The two texts that
  no baseline shows (`enable_workspace`, `last_role`) stay unpinned, as the B4a
  review found. The refusal texts of the form (`env_secret_note`,
  `env_reference`) are asserted by the tests through the same data.
- The hardware cursor column of a baseline depends on the last frame that wrote
  to the terminal. `jackin_fixed_env_form` is captured after a frame that only
  moves the gutter, because the frame right after Tab left the edited Key input
  gave column 48 on a quiet machine and 80 when the machine was loaded.

## B4a fix round: the UX and red/green review findings

Reviews: `P9.3-B4a-ux.md` (CHANGES_REQUIRED) and `P9.3-B4a-redgreen.md`
(CHANGES_REQUIRED). The fixes are in commits `b53a4f9` (UX) and `4d68656`
(tests). Run them with:

```sh
cargo nextest run --locked -p jackin-console --test functional -E 'test(jackin_fixed_editor) | test(jackin_fixed_dirty_exit) | test(jackin_fixed_project_row) | test(jackin_fixed_workspace_account) | test(jackin_fixed_last_allowed_role)'
```

| Finding | Fix | Test or baseline | Break |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| UX F1 | the Project row no longer advertises `o Open source` | `jackin_fixed_project_row_keeps_its_paint_and_refuses_every_edit`, `jackin_fixed_editor_project` (row 39 only) | FX1 |
| UX F2 | `fixed()` enables every account | `jackin_fixed_editor_accounts` (row 9 only) | FX2 |
| UX D1, D2 | `last_role` is `Keep one role allowed · allow another first`; `enable_workspace` is `All accounts are active here · press p to prefer one` | none shows them in a frame | none, see below |
| RG row 3 | the footer of the focused fixed select has no `Space Toggle` | `jackin_fixed_dirty_exit_refuses_the_list_by_key_and_click` | FX3 |
| RG row 5 | the key loop adds `n` and `delete`; the three contrast assertions are deleted, the Add row assertion stays | `jackin_fixed_project_row_keeps_its_paint_and_refuses_every_edit` | FX4, FX5 |
| RG row 7 | a stale role name next to `backend` does not count as an allowed role | `jackin_fixed_last_allowed_role_cannot_be_unchecked` (the separate test was merged into it in the B4b fix round) | FX6 |
| UX F3 | a click on the last allowed role is refused | `jackin_fixed_last_allowed_role_refuses_a_click` | FX7 |

The two new tests are plan rows added in the fix round, for test-reviewer
judgement: the stale name is the T26 case "a name in the list that is no longer
a configured role counts as not allowed" (`editor_refuses_last_allowed_role`),
and the click is T21 assertion 2 for the last-role refusal.

Not covered by a break:

- The two reworded reasons. The tests read them from `Fixed::openshell()` and no
  frame shows them, so a typo in either is not caught here (reviewer D5, D6b).
- The three deleted contrast assertions. The reviewer showed that no break ever
  turned them red and that the frozen `jackin_fixed_editor_project` baseline
  catches a dim row (F1 to F10 of the review).
- Not done, left for later steps: UX F4 (post the reason on the first click of a
  fixed row), UX D3 (muted ink for L1 reasons), the Project row context menu
  items that only refuse, and the sticky-status gap of the B4a record.

## B4b fix round: the UX and red/green review findings

Reviews: `P9.3-B4b-ux.md` (CHANGES_REQUIRED, six required changes) and
`P9.3-B4b-redgreen.md` (CHANGES_REQUIRED, four required changes). Decision
D27 holds: no approved baseline changes. The three `jackin_fixed_*` baselines
that move are new in this unmerged branch, and
`git diff --name-status 00031c0..HEAD -- '*baselines*'` still lists only `A`
lines. Run the tests with:

```sh
cargo nextest run --locked -p jackin-console --test functional -E 'test(jackin_fixed)'
cargo nextest run --locked -p jackin-console --test fixed_settings --test vocabulary
```

| Finding | Fix | Test or baseline | Break |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| UX F1 | the reason under the source radio is `Tone::Muted` | `jackin_fixed_env_form` (row 17), contrast assertion in `jackin_fixed_env_sources_refuse_the_radio_options_and_the_p_key` | BF4 |
| UX F2 | the 1Password option stays visible and fixed, labelled `1Password item / field` (`Vocabulary.env_op_label`; the demo keeps its text) | `jackin_fixed_env_form` (row 14), the env sources test, `the_openshell_vocabulary_renames_the_demo_words` | BF3 |
| UX F3 | `env_reference` is `Plain values only · use an account for credentials`; the test waits for the error row `! <text>` because the same text is already on screen as the source note | `jackin_fixed_env_form_refuses_a_credential_reference_and_notes_a_secret_name` | BF7 |
| UX F4 | the Trust hint is `o Show source` (`Vocabulary.trust_hint`; the demo keeps `o Open source`) | `jackin_fixed_settings_trust` (row 39), `the_demo_settings_keep_every_control` | BF1, BF2 |
| UX F5 | the value input is required: `Value for the key *`, `Required` on an empty Save | `jackin_fixed_env_form` (row 19), `the_demo_env_form_requires_a_value` | BF5 |
| UX F6 | `save_env` checks for a duplicate before it touches `pending`; the unreachable `cfg.env` `Form` arm is deleted | `a_rejected_rename_leaves_the_original_variable_alone`, `the_demo_env_form_stays_open_on_a_rejected_rename_and_changes_nothing` | BF6 |
| Keep-open | recorded in `docs/current-state.md` as a deliberate demo change on the error path | the two rename tests and the reference test | BF8 |
| UX D1 | `env_sources` and `env_reference` are one string; the first secret note line is `Keep secrets out of env. Values are stored as plain text.` | `jackin_fixed_env_form` (row 17), `every_fixed_text_fits_the_surface_that_shows_it` | BF10, BF11 |
| UX D2 | the Agents detail says `no credentials handed to the sandbox` (`Vocabulary.agent_no_credentials`; the demo keeps `container`) | `jackin_fixed_settings_agents` (rows 8, 10 to 13) | BF9 |
| RG row 5 | the role overrides test waits for the `Scope` field and its value row | `jackin_fixed_settings_role_overrides_refuse_and_the_scope_lists_global` | BF12 |
| RG row 7 | the credential reference test waits for both note lines in one predicate | `jackin_fixed_env_form_refuses_a_credential_reference_and_notes_a_secret_name` | BF13 |
| RG row 4 | the agents test presses `c` and waits for the Accounts page | `jackin_fixed_settings_agents_refuse_every_row` | BF14 |
| RG row 21 | `jackin_fixed_last_allowed_role_cannot_be_unchecked` shows `ghost-role` checked and `not in registry` before it moves on; the separate stale-name test is deleted | `jackin_fixed_last_allowed_role_cannot_be_unchecked` | BF15, BF16 (FX6) |

Re-accepted baselines, by cell diff against the committed file (text, colours
and attributes of every cell; the cursor is unchanged in all three):

| Baseline | Rows that change |
| --- | --- |
| `jackin_fixed_settings_trust` | 39: `o Open source` to `o Show source` |
| `jackin_fixed_env_form` | 14: the option label loses `  (recommended for secrets)`. 17: the note reads `Plain values only · use an account for credentials` in `8a8a8a` instead of `737373`. 19: `Value for the key  optional` to `Value for the key *` |
| `jackin_fixed_settings_agents` | 8, 10, 11, 12, 13: `container` to `sandbox` (the only ink change is on the two cells that the shorter word leaves blank) |

No approved baseline is touched. The value input is required in the demo form
too, which no approved baseline shows (the 15 approved baselines replay green).

What the records show and what they cannot:

- The torn-frame race of rows 5 and 7 cannot be turned red by a one-edit change
  to the code: the old waits failed once in 600 runs under load, because they
  read a half-drawn dialog. BF12 and BF13 show that the new waits are selective:
  they time out with a message that names the missing part. The proof of the
  race fix is the stress run: the six changed PTY tests, 30 iterations at
  `-j 4` (`cargo nextest run --locked -j 4 -p jackin-console --test functional
  --stress-count 30 -E '<the six tests>'`), `30/30 stress run iterations: 30
  passed`, exit 0.
- BF14 is the reviewer's break A8 and BF15 is FXD. Both stayed green against the
  old tests and are red against the fixed ones.
- The test `the_demo_env_form_keeps_an_empty_value_open_with_its_message` and
  its record DM5 are gone. With the input required, Save on an empty value stops
  at the form and never reaches `save_env`, so removing `keep_open_on_save` no
  longer turns that test red. The keep-open behaviour is pinned by the two
  rejected-rename tests and by the reference test (BF8). The
  `Value cannot be empty` check in `save_env` stays as the review asked, as a
  backstop that no key or click reaches.
- The assertion that the two secret note lines together fit 64 columns is
  deleted. It fitted the old single sentence; the lines are independent now and
  each is still held to 62 columns (BF10).
- The new wording of `env_secret_note[1]`, `env_sources` and `env_reference` is
  not pinned beyond the frozen frames: the tests read those texts from
  `Fixed::openshell()`. `env_secret_note` has no frame.
- BF16 is the former FX6, rerun against the merged test. ER1 was rerun and ER2 is
  the break of BF8; both now quote the new error row text. TX5 and the older records that quote texts of
  earlier rounds are history.

## B1b: packaging, one app and two modes (BX22, G1 to G5)

Tests: `apps/jackin/tests/product_mode.rs`, `product_manifest.rs`,
`library_has_no_demo.rs`. Plan: test plan v7.1 delta T34 BL, with the
review amendments N1 and N2 (and A1 to A5 of the v7 review).

Differences from the plan text, each decided while building:

- `product_manifest` has two tests, not five (N2 preferred). `manifest_shape`
  collects every broken rule and prints all of them; each plausible edit of
  the manifest is one record below, and each makes it red. The second test,
  `product_mode_test_is_gated_on_the_demo_being_off`, is N1: it reads the
  first line of `tests/product_mode.rs`.
- `product_mode` has two tests. `assemble`, `Entry` and `AppOptions` do not
  exist before B2c, so the test builds the app with `App::for_scenario`, and
  the test `Backend` and `OnePassword` implement the full trait (about 90
  lines, not 30). The methods the manager never calls are `unreachable!`.
  The second test covers the About rows from `Surfaces.about`.
- `library_has_no_demo` scans an in-memory list of `(path, text)` pairs
  (`scan(&[Source], pending)`), so the fixture trees need no temporary
  directory. The real-tree test reads `apps/jackin/src`. `demo_modules_are_cfg_gated`
  reads the real `lib.rs`; its negative cases are the separate test
  `an_ungated_demo_module_is_reported`.
- The `1Password locked: unlock the app and retry` literal is scoped to
  `launch.rs`, because `accounts.rs` and `backend.rs` carry the same
  sentence as real domain text.
- The breaks of the scanner tests change the scanner in the test file
  itself (it is the code under test); the other breaks change library
  source or the manifest.

Reproduce one record (the product-shape tests use
`--no-default-features --features product` instead of `--all-features`):

```sh
cargo nextest run --locked --no-fail-fast -p jackin-console --all-features \
  --test <file> -E 'test(=<name>)'
```

### B1b evidence (shell commands, not tests)

Test counts, `cargo nextest list --locked --workspace --all-features`:
282 before B1b, 293 after (exit 0). `diff` of the sorted lists shows 11
added names and 0 removed: 9 in `library_has_no_demo`, 2 in
`product_manifest`. `product_mode` is gated off under `--all-features`.

Product shape, `cargo nextest list --locked -p jackin-console
--no-default-features --features product`: 13 tests, 9 `library_has_no_demo`,
2 `product_manifest`, 2 `product_mode` (the `cfg` line of `product_mode.rs`
is therefore live; `product_mode_test_is_gated_on_the_demo_being_off` keeps
it so).

The silently skipped suite: `cargo nextest list --locked --workspace`
(default features, no flag) lists 151 tests. The 144 `jackin-console`
tests that need the demo are absent from it, without a failure. This is why
every gate command names `--all-features`.

```text
$ cargo build --locked -p jackin-console --no-default-features
    Finished `dev` profile [unoptimized + debuginfo] target(s)   (exit 0)

$ cargo tree --locked -p jackin-console --no-default-features -e normal -i clap
error: package ID specification `clap` did not match any packages   (exit 101)

$ cargo tree --locked -p jackin-console --features demo -e normal -i clap
clap v4.6.7
├── jackin-console v0.1.0
└── junie-tui v0.1.0
```

`cli` feature) uses `clap`; `jackin-console` has no edge to it.

CI wiring, `velnor-actions` 0.1.72 (pinned in `mise.toml`):
`velnor-actions generate` (exit 0), then `velnor-actions plan` (exit 0)
lists four Rust jobs: `rust-jackin-console`, `rust-jackin-console-product`,
`rust-junie-tui`, `rust-junie-tui-product`. The argv in
`.github/velnor/task-execution-manifest-v1.json`:

```text

stack/rust/apps/jackin/nextest/default  nextest run ... --package jackin-console --no-tests fail --all-features
stack/rust/apps/jackin/build/default    nextest list ... --all-features
stack/rust/apps/jackin/doc/default      doc ... --all-features
stack/rust/apps/jackin/clippy/product   clippy ... --package jackin-console --all-targets --no-default-features --features product -- -D warnings
stack/rust/apps/jackin/nextest/product  nextest run ... --package jackin-console --no-tests fail --no-default-features --features product
stack/rust/apps/jackin/build/product    nextest list ... --no-default-features --features product
stack/rust/apps/jackin/doc/product      doc ... --no-default-features --features product
stack/rust/root/*/default               the same four with --all-features
stack/rust/root/*/product               the same four with no feature flag (junie-tui does not declare product)
```

Job names in `ci.yml`: `Rust / jackin-console`, `Rust / jackin-console (product)`,
`Rust / junie-tui`, `Rust / junie-tui (product)`; `required` lists all four in
`needs`. `suite-functional` keeps `nextest run --workspace --no-tests fail
--profile functional` with no feature flag (suites have no feature field).

The first full demo run ended 292 passed, 1 failed:
`jackin::jackin_first_use_empty_manager_and_intro` timed out after 11.4 s
waiting for `Current directory` while the intro rain was still on screen.
The host load average was 73 (other sessions). Three solo reruns of that
test passed (12 to 13 s each), and the second full run passed 293 of 293.
No code of this step touches the intro; the failure is the 10 s observation
deadline under load.

## B2c: Entry and AppOptions replace Scenario (BX18, G6, G17)

Tests: `apps/jackin/tests/entry.rs` (six tests). Plan: test plan v7.1 delta
T35, with review amendment N5 (the native sink wording is tested once, here,
and is deleted from T49). N17 (`console_fixture_args`) belongs to the jackin
step J1 and is not part of this step. The step moves `Scenario`, `CLOCK` and
`entry_for` into `fixtures`, so the `PENDING` list of `library_has_no_demo`
loses its `Scenario` and `CLOCK` rows; the two records at the end of this
section show the scanner rejecting both names again.

Differences from the plan text, each decided while building:

- `native_sink_decides_the_wording` and `clipboard_modes` copy the container
  row of the capsule Debug info dialog (`ctrl+b i`, then `y`), so the
  accepted wording is `Copied 29 characters · jackin-payments-platform-7f3a`,
  not `Copied selection`. A mouse drag over a script pane in the capsule
  forms no selection under `Application::handle` (the pane shows no
  selection after Down, Drag, Drag, Up). The commit before this step does the
  same (checked on a copy of the tree), so it is not a change of this step.
  `Copied selection` is the same `Clipboard::report` branch with
  `what = Some("selection")` and is not exercised here.
- With `ClipboardMode::Preview` the plan says the sink gets nothing. A sink
  cannot be attached to `Preview`, so the test asserts what can differ: no
  OSC 52 string reaches `Application::take_output`.
- `start_table_matches_the_old_scenarios` has 19 rows for the 15 scenarios
  (`first-use` twice, the launch and outro scenarios at frames 0 and 30). The
  expected routes and texts were checked against the commit before this step:
  a throwaway test printed route, screen length and byte sum of the
  140 by 40 screen for all 15 scenarios at frames 0 and 30 on both trees, and
  the 30 lines are identical.
- `hard_cases_credentials_plan` drives the manager launch picker with real
  keys under `Motion::Full` and ticks (150) until the credentials dialog
  appears. Its negatives are a signed-in 1Password session and the
  `Claude · Personal` row.
- `LaunchEntry` is a type alias of `LaunchTarget` (the argument of
  `Backend::launch_plan`); the two designed structs have the same four
  fields. `NativeClipboard` lives in `junie_tui::shell`, where
  `Clipboard::copy` is, not in `jackin_console`.
- `fixtures::app_with_world` exists next to `app_for` for tests that change
  the world before they build the app. `app_for` calls it, so there is one
  construction path.
- The command of the plan, `rg -w Scenario apps/jackin/src --glob
  '!fixtures/**' --glob '!bin/**'`, matches the globs against the working
  directory, so from the repository root it still lists `fixtures/`. Run from
  `apps/jackin/src`, or with `--glob '!**/fixtures/**'`, it has 0 hits.
- Two existing tests changed beyond the mechanical edit.
  `snapshot::apply_leaves_the_console_state_alone` loses its
  `target.scenario` line, and
  `world::every_scenario_builds_a_world_that_names_it` reads the About row
  `Scenario`, because `World.scenario` no longer exists.

Reproduce one record:

```sh
cargo nextest run --locked --no-fail-fast -p jackin-console --all-features \
  --test <file> -E 'test(=<name>)'
```

The `real_tree_is_clean` records use `--no-default-features --features
product` instead of `--all-features`. D3 first ran with `is_none_or` in
place of `is_some_and`; that run stayed green because no row of the test has
an absent account, so the break was replaced by `is_some()`, which goes red.

### B2c evidence (shell commands, not tests)

Test counts, `cargo nextest list --locked --workspace --all-features`: 293
before B2c, 299 after. The sorted lists differ by the six names of
`entry` and nothing else.

Visual identity (design 4.6 item 6): `git status --porcelain --
tests/functional/baselines apps/jackin/tests/functional/baselines` prints
nothing after the full demo run, and every functional journey of the 15
scenarios ends in its frozen `App::visual` check, so each approved baseline
matched.

Route and screen fingerprints of the commit before B2c and of B2c, from a
throwaway test (not committed): for each of the 15 scenarios at frames 0
and 30, the route, the length and the byte sum of the 140 by 40 screen.
The 30 lines are identical (`diff` exit 0).
